Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the mass_spec object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 476. The moduli space has dimension at most 434 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to mass_spec theory, we decompose the problem into 476 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 434. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
